   65:     """Injetável por teste -- mesmo padrão de ``fetch_fii``/etc em
   66:     ``refresh_portfolio``."""
   67: 
   68:     fetch_fii: object = None
   69:     fetch_etf: object = None
   70:     fetch_fixed_income: object = None
   71:     fetch_equity: object = None
   72:     fetch_fiagro: object = None
   73:     analyzers: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
   74:     knowledge_bridge_cls: object = None
   75: 
   76: 
   77: def analyze_portfolio(
   78:     *,
   79:     bolsai_api_key: str | None,
   80:     brapi_token: str | None,
   81:     vault_path: str,
   82:     ano: int | None = None,
   83:     mes: int | None = None,
   84:     positions: tuple[PortfolioAsset, ...] | None = None,
   85:     deps: _BatchDeps | None = None,
   86: ) -> AnalysisRunResult:
   87:     """Busca dado real, roda o analisador certo, e persiste no vault --
   88:     para toda posição que tem sector/industry reais disponíveis.
   89:     """
   90:     import datetime as _dt
   91: 
   92:     from iip.analysis import AssetData
   93:     from iip.cli.fetch_template import (
   94:         fetch_equity_template_live,
   95:         fetch_etf_template_live,
   96:         fetch_fiagro_template_live,
   97:         fetch_fii_template_live,
   98:         fetch_fixed_income_template_live,
   99:     )
  100:     from iip.cli.main import ANALYZERS
  101:     from iip.knowledge.bridge import KnowledgeBridge
  102: 
  103:     deps = deps or _BatchDeps()
  104:     fetch_fii = deps.fetch_fii or fetch_fii_template_live
  105:     fetch_etf = deps.fetch_etf or fetch_etf_template_live
  106:     fetch_fixed_income = deps.fetch_fixed_income or fetch_fixed_income_template_live
  107:     fetch_equity = deps.fetch_equity or fetch_equity_template_live
  108:     fetch_fiagro = deps.fetch_fiagro or fetch_fiagro_template_live
  109:     analyzers = deps.analyzers or ANALYZERS
  110:     knowledge_bridge_cls = deps.knowledge_bridge_cls or KnowledgeBridge
  111: 
  112:     hoje = _dt.date.today()  # noqa: DTZ011 — data de calendário (referência CVM), não timestamp
  113:     ano_efetivo = ano or hoje.year
  114:     mes_efetivo = mes or hoje.month
  115: 
  116:     all_positions = positions if positions is not None else assets_refreshable_now()
  117:     bridge = knowledge_bridge_cls(vault_path)
  118: 
  119:     outcomes: list[PositionOutcome] = []
  120:     for position in all_positions:
  121:         template_type = _template_type_for(position)
  122:         if template_type is None:
  123:             outcomes.append(
  124:                 PositionOutcome(
  125:                     ticker=position.ticker,
  126:                     status="pulado",
  127:                     detail="classe de ativo sem fetch automático ainda",
  128:                 )
  129:             )
  130:             continue
  131: 
  132:         sector, industry = _sector_industry_for(position)
  133:         if not sector or not industry:
  134:             outcomes.append(
  135:                 PositionOutcome(
  136:                     ticker=position.ticker,
  137:                     status="pulado",
  138:                     detail=(
  139:                         "sem sector/industry reais no registro — preencha "
  140:                         "PortfolioAsset.sector/.industry (ou "
  141:                         ".structure/.segment pra fundos) antes de analisar"
  142:                     ),
  143:                 )
  144:             )
  145:             continue
  146: 
  147:         try:
  148:             if template_type == "fii":
  149:                 template, _ = fetch_fii(
  150:                     position.ticker, position.cnpj, ano_efetivo, bolsai_api_key
  151:                 )
  152:             elif template_type == "etf":
  153:                 template, _ = fetch_etf(
  154:                     position.ticker, position.cnpj, ano_efetivo, mes_efetivo, brapi_token
  155:                 )
  156:             elif template_type == "equity":
  157:                 template, _ = fetch_equity(position.ticker, bolsai_api_key, brapi_token)
  158:             elif template_type == "fiagro":
  159:                 template, _ = fetch_fiagro(
  160:                     position.ticker, position.cnpj, ano_efetivo, mes_efetivo, brapi_token
  161:                 )
  162:             else:  # fixed_income
  163:                 template, _ = fetch_fixed_income(
  164:                     position.ticker, position.cnpj, ano_efetivo, mes_efetivo
  165:                 )
  166:         except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — isolamento por posição, mesmo padrão de refresh_portfolio
  167:             outcomes.append(
  168:                 PositionOutcome(ticker=position.ticker, status="erro", detail=str(exc))
  169:             )
  170:             continue
  171: 
  172:         analyzer_type = _TEMPLATE_TYPE_TO_ANALYZER_TYPE[template_type]
  173:         data = AssetData(
  174:             symbol=position.ticker,
  175:             sector=sector,
  176:             industry=industry,
  177:             market_cap=template.get("market_cap"),
  178:             price=template.get("price"),
  179:             financials=template.get("financials", {}),
  180:         )
  181: 
  182:         try:
  183:             report = analyzers[analyzer_type]().analyze(data)
  184:             resultado_persist = bridge.sync_analysis_projection(
  185:                 report, position.ticker, analyzer_type
  186:             )
  187:         except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — isolamento por posição, mesmo padrão de refresh_portfolio
  188:             outcomes.append(
  189:                 PositionOutcome(ticker=position.ticker, status="erro", detail=str(exc))
  190:             )
  191:             continue
  192: 
  193:         outcomes.append(
  194:             PositionOutcome(
  195:                 ticker=position.ticker,
  196:                 status="ok",
  197:                 detail=f"{resultado_persist.status.value} — {resultado_persist.path}",
  198:             )
  199:         )
  200: 
  201:     return AnalysisRunResult(outcomes=tuple(outcomes))

FILE: src\iip\portfolio\refresh.py
    1: """Batch-refresh every portfolio position this project can fetch data for.
    2: 
    3: Reuses ``iip.cli.fetch_template.fetch_fii_template_live`` /
    4: ``fetch_etf_template_live`` / ``fetch_fixed_income_template_live`` /
    5: ``fetch_equity_template_live`` / ``fetch_fiagro_template_live`` per
    6: position — no new fetching logic here, just the loop, per-position
    7: error isolation (one bad position must not abort the whole run), and
    8: dated snapshot output.
    9: 
   10: ``fund``/``etf``/``fixed_income`` positions need a verified CNPJ (see
   11: ``iip.portfolio.registry.assets_with_cnpj``); ``equity`` positions
   12: don't (fetched by ticker via bolsai/brapi, not by CNPJ) — see
   13: ``iip.portfolio.registry.assets_refreshable_now`` for the combined
   14: set this module refreshes by default. Positions outside these classes
   15: are skipped with a clear reason, never silently guessed.
   16: """
   17: 
   18: from __future__ import annotations
   19: 
   20: import datetime as _dt
   21: import json
   22: from dataclasses import dataclass, field
   23: from pathlib import Path
   24: 
   25: from iip.portfolio.registry import PortfolioAsset, assets_refreshable_now
   26: 
   27: 
   28: @dataclass(frozen=True)
   29: class PositionOutcome:
   30:     ticker: str
   31:     status: str  # "ok", "erro", "pulado"
   32:     detail: str
   33:     fetched_fields: tuple[str, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)
   34: 
   35: 
   36: @dataclass(frozen=True)
   37: class RefreshRunResult:
   38:     run_date: str
   39:     outcomes: tuple[PositionOutcome, ...]
   40: 
   41:     @property
   42:     def succeeded(self) -> tuple[PositionOutcome, ...]:
   43:         return tuple(o for o in self.outcomes if o.status == "ok")
   44: 
   45:     @property
   46:     def failed(self) -> tuple[PositionOutcome, ...]:
   47:         return tuple(o for o in self.outcomes if o.status == "erro")
   48: 
   49:     @property
   50:     def skipped(self) -> tuple[PositionOutcome, ...]:
   51:         return tuple(o for o in self.outcomes if o.status == "pulado")
   52: 
   53: 
   54: def _template_type_for(position: PortfolioAsset) -> str | None:
   55:     """Which live-fetch function a position should use.
   56: 
   57:     Real bug found and fixed here (12/09/2026): ``asset_class="fund"``
   58:     was routing EVERY subtype through the FII-specific CVM Informe
   59:     Mensal — but FI-Infra positions (CDII11, JURO11, CPTI11) are NOT
   60:     registered under that regulatory category at all (confirmed live:
   61:     CDII11's real CNPJ does not appear in the FII dataset). They ARE
   62:     registered as general ICVM 555 funds (confirmed live: CDII11's
   63:     CNPJ DOES appear in the Informe Diário) — the same dataset ETF and
   64:     fixed_income already use. Before this fix, ``refresh-portfolio``
   65:     reported "ok" for these positions while silently finding zero real
   66:     data, since an empty CNPJ match doesn't raise an error, just
   67:     leaves fields at their defaults with a buried warning.
   68: 
   69:     FI-Agro (CRAA11) needed a THIRD dataset (confirmed live: its CNPJ
   70:     appears in neither the FII nor the Informe Diário dataset) — CVM's
   71:     own dedicated FIAGRO Informe Mensal, now wired via
   72:     ``fetch_fiagro_template_live``.
   73:     """
   74:     if position.asset_class == "fund":
   75:         if position.subtype == "FI-Infra":
   76:             return "fixed_income"
   77:         if position.subtype == "FI-Agro":
   78:             return "fiagro"
   79:         return "fii"
   80:     if position.asset_class in ("etf", "fixed_income", "equity"):
   81:         return position.asset_class
   82:     return None
   83: 
   84: 
   85: def _refreshable_positions(
   86:     positions: tuple[PortfolioAsset, ...],
   87: ) -> tuple[PortfolioAsset, ...]:
   88:     return tuple(p for p in positions if _template_type_for(p) is not None)
   89: 
   90: 
   91: def refresh_portfolio(
   92:     output_dir: Path,
   93:     *,
   94:     bolsai_api_key: str | None,
   95:     brapi_token: str | None,
   96:     ano: int | None = None,
   97:     mes: int | None = None,
   98:     positions: tuple[PortfolioAsset, ...] | None = None,
   99:     fetch_fii=None,
  100:     fetch_etf=None,
  101:     fetch_fixed_income=None,
  102:     fetch_equity=None,
  103:     fetch_fiagro=None,
  104: ) -> RefreshRunResult:
  105:     """Refresh every refreshable position, writing one JSON snapshot per
  106:     ticker under ``output_dir/{data}/{ticker}.json``.
  107: 
  108:     ``fetch_fii``/``fetch_etf``/``fetch_fixed_income``/``fetch_equity``/
  109:     ``fetch_fiagro`` are injectable (default to the real live-fetch
  110:     functions) purely for testability — same pattern as the
  111:     harvesters' injectable ``opener``.
  112:     """
  113:     from iip.cli.fetch_template import (
  114:         fetch_equity_template_live,
  115:         fetch_etf_template_live,
  116:         fetch_fiagro_template_live,
  117:         fetch_fii_template_live,
  118:         fetch_fixed_income_template_live,
  119:     )
  120: 
  121:     fetch_fii = fetch_fii or fetch_fii_template_live
  122:     fetch_etf = fetch_etf or fetch_etf_template_live
  123:     fetch_fixed_income = fetch_fixed_income or fetch_fixed_income_template_live
  124:     fetch_equity = fetch_equity or fetch_equity_template_live
  125:     fetch_fiagro = fetch_fiagro or fetch_fiagro_template_live
  126: 
  127:     hoje = _dt.date.today()  # noqa: DTZ011 — data de calendário (data de referência do snapshot), não timestamp
  128:     ano_efetivo = ano or hoje.year
  129:     mes_efetivo = mes or hoje.month
  130:     run_date = hoje.isoformat()
  131: 
  132:     all_positions = positions if positions is not None else assets_refreshable_now()
  133:     refreshable = _refreshable_positions(all_positions)
  134: 
  135:     snapshot_dir = output_dir / run_date
  136:     snapshot_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  137: 
  138:     outcomes: list[PositionOutcome] = []
  139:     for position in refreshable:
  140:         template_type = _template_type_for(position)
  141:         try:
  142:             if template_type == "fii":
  143:                 template, resultado = fetch_fii(
  144:                     position.ticker, position.cnpj, ano_efetivo, bolsai_api_key
  145:                 )
  146:             elif template_type == "etf":
  147:                 template, resultado = fetch_etf(
  148:                     position.ticker,
  149:                     position.cnpj,
  150:                     ano_efetivo,
  151:                     mes_efetivo,
  152:                     brapi_token,
  153:                 )
  154:             elif template_type == "equity":
  155:                 template, resultado = fetch_equity(
  156:                     position.ticker, bolsai_api_key, brapi_token
  157:                 )
  158:             elif template_type == "fiagro":
  159:                 template, resultado = fetch_fiagro(
  160:                     position.ticker,
  161:                     position.cnpj,
  162:                     ano_efetivo,
  163:                     mes_efetivo,
  164:                     brapi_token,
  165:                 )
  166:             else:  # fixed_income
  167:                 template, resultado = fetch_fixed_income(
  168:                     position.ticker,
  169:                     position.cnpj,
  170:                     ano_efetivo,
  171:                     mes_efetivo,
  172:                 )
  173:         except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — isolamento por posição, ver docstring do módulo
  174:             outcomes.append(
  175:                 PositionOutcome(
  176:                     ticker=position.ticker,
  177:                     status="erro",
  178:                     detail=str(exc),
  179:                 )
  180:             )
  181:             continue
  182: 
  183:         snapshot_path = snapshot_dir / f"{position.ticker}.json"
  184:         snapshot_path.write_text(
  185:             json.dumps(template, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8"
  186:         )
  187:         outcomes.append(
  188:             PositionOutcome(
  189:                 ticker=position.ticker,
  190:                 status="ok",
  191:                 detail=str(snapshot_path),
  192:                 fetched_fields=resultado.fetched_fields,
  193:             )
  194:         )
  195: 
  196:     skipped_tickers = {p.ticker for p in all_positions} - {p.ticker for p in refreshable}
  197:     for ticker in sorted(skipped_tickers):
  198:         outcomes.append(
  199:             PositionOutcome(
  200:                 ticker=ticker,
  201:                 status="pulado",
  202:                 detail="classe de ativo sem fetch automático ainda (só fund/etf/fixed_income/equity/fiagro)",
  203:             )
  204:         )
  205: 
  206:     return RefreshRunResult(run_date=run_date, outcomes=tuple(outcomes))

FILE: src\iip\portfolio_data\refresh.py

    4:

    6:

   11:     source: str
   12:     priority: int
   13: 
   14: 
   15: def prioritize(tasks: tuple[RefreshTask, ...]) -> tuple[RefreshTask, ...]:
   16:     return tuple(sorted(tasks, key=lambda t: (t.priority, t.ticker)))

    6: def test_existing_evidence_does_not_block_projection():

--- tests\test_atlas_knowledge_idempotency.py | linha 11 | símbolos: persist_evidence ---
    7:     class Bridge:
    8:         def __init__(self):
    9:             self.sync_calls = 0
   10: 
   11:         def persist_evidence(self, evidence):
   12:             raise FileExistsError("append-only")
   13: 
   14:         def sync_evidence_projection(self, evidence):
   15:             self.sync_calls += 1
   16:             return type(
   17:                 "Result", (), {"status": type("Status", (), {"value": "UNCHANGED"})()}
